_Roughly 14:53-20:30 is an all-English planning talk (AI tools, tests); 12:27-13:25, 20:31-30:23, 36:37-42:59 and 45:18-1:00:46 are Latin-transliterated, often garbled (e.g. 25:05-25:38 'ana bihna binkhales al-asal', 'ahana shay na'na' ma asal mufrad' could not be parsed into rows). 22:49-24:55 is a honey seller at the door (not Amal). Speaker labels are fragmented; many '?' lines were assigned from flow (e.g. 02:31 'or يتعلّم', 34:00 'هو بتيبست' self-fixed). The lesson is one long drill on بسّط/انبسط conjugations, so grammar rows dominate and vocab rows are few. Left out as S4 pronunciation: لقة/luka for لغة (00:39), بسّتني for بسطني (30:35), بيسبت for بيبسط (32:30), بنسبت for بنبسط (34:21), bansebet for banbeset (56:04), بيسبط for ببسّط (1:02:23), maruj for mahruj (22:09), 'ma' for ma3 (25:38, 26:19), stress on the T (55:25). Self-fixes not logged: أكتب→احكي (05:28), سميح→مبسوط (27:54), basati→basatini (41:09), بساتِتني→بساتِتك (35:58). English words inside Arabic not on her sheet, so not tier 3: software, research, possibility, test (she uses 'test' herself), mispronounce, AI. 26:19 'aswad' read as aswa2 = worse (on sheet), so not a vocab error. / Diarization fragmented: many Medi lines split across '?' fragments and some Amal/Medi labels swapped (e.g. 09:34-09:38 'Al AI مرات مش ناجح' labelled Amal is Medi). _Lines 1-90 are Arabic script; from ~05:00 the transcript is Latin transliteration. 19:20-31:30 has many Medi/Amal Arabic words dropped entirely (blank gaps: 'So can, can', 'Is it or-', 'be as'), so the flight/meetings/parents/'I made him smile' drills could not be checked and rows 20-21 are low confidence. Not written as errors: pronunciation/vowel repeats (01:52 أتمارنت, 04:27 أزرق, 09:13 kuffet->khuffet, 46:13 sawa->sawwa, 53:44 and 58:42 double-T commands), factual time fixes at 03:41-04:11 (2 o'clock, 26 min), his own self-fix 03:02 مختلفة, listening comprehension 56:07 him/her, and his unfinished 1:00:54 'il film mbare' which she completed in chat at 1:04:26. medi_turns counts every Medi line with an Arabic attempt (script or Latin), excluding pure filler. / Arabic script only for 00:13-04:53; from 05:00 to the end the engine wrote Latin transliteration, and between roughly 19:30 and 31:30 it dropped many of Medi's and Amal's Arabic words entirely (lines like 'So is it ?', 'Is [ ] I am tired'), so several fixes there (show made me laugh, work made me tired, he's funny, he makes me bored, I made him smile, meetings make me bored, my parents made me upset, the flight made me tired) can only be inferred from Amal's English prompts and her later chat lines; those rows are marked low. Speaker labels looked right throughout. Not filed: clock corrections at 03:41 (tlaat -> tentain) and 04:02 (minutes -> sitte u 3eshreen) are fact fixes, not language; pronunciation-only fixes skipped per S4 (02:17 atmaarant -> atmarrant vowel length, 09:13 kuffet -> khuffet, 46:13 sawa vowel length, 53:59/58:57 double-T simplification ma etta3ebhom / ma tta3bina, 04:31 azra2 repeat). Medi's own free sentence at 1:00:54 'il film mbare' was abandoned and only completed in chat (el-filem mbaare7 da77akni). 31:24 'I made them scared / khawafatum' for her prompt 'him' was accepted by Amal and her chat also has -hom, so not filed._

Not errors: 'crispy' (52:11, she said not learned); pizza/pepperoni/bacon/croissant/chocolate/cappuccino/credit card/holes/fire department/ambulance not on her sheet (she uses several herself); 31:16 'A shawarma' is a whole English clause. 11:46 'أسبوع شغل' is most likely her 'usboo3 shoab' misheard by the ASR, not a vocab slip. 47:27 'أرجع أحط' after بعدين left unflagged because her own chat keeps arja3. medi_turns counts Medi lines carrying Arabic script or Latin-transliterated Arabic._
